- **iso9000-agentops**: 6-Echelon ISO 9001:2015, ISO/IEC 42001:2023 & Agile V Quality Pipeline with Bidirectional RTM, ISO 31000 FMEA RPN Engine, HMAC Gate 1 Seals, Self-Grading Bias Guard, Planarian Rollback, and SARIF 2.1.0 Quality Certificates. ([orakulzero/iso9000-agentops](https://github.com/orakulzero/iso9000-agentops/tree/HEAD/skills/iso9000-agentops) · ★1)
- **iso9001-qms-generator**: 基于过程和风险思维生成符合ISO9001标准的质量管理体系文件，支持四级文件结构和乌龟图；覆盖质量手册、程序文件、作业文件、记录表单等全套体系文件｜联系方式：[email removed] ([canquedehuiyi-cpu/xiongbao-nenglishichang](https://github.com/canquedehuiyi-cpu/xiongbao-nenglishichang/tree/HEAD/skills/market/Product-Management/iso9001-qms-generator))
- **isobaric-compound-mass-matching**: Use when assessing the robustness of metabolomics ORA results to metabolite misidentification: you have a differential metabolite list, a background compound set, and KEGG pathway sets, and need to quantify pathway loss/gain under simulated misidentification at a chosen rate (e.g. 4%). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isobaric-compound-mass-matching))
- **isobaric-ion-detection-msi**: Use when you have loaded MSI data with an extracted peak list and need to annotate matrix-related signals, particularly when the dataset may contain isobaric ions or peaks with overlapping spatial distributions that could be misclassified during downstream annotation filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isobaric-ion-detection-msi))
- **isobaric-ion-misclassification-documentation**: Use when after matrix annotation has been performed on mass spectrometry imaging (MSI) data when you need to identify and document ions whose m/z values overlap with or are isobaric to other peaks, creating risk of false positive or false negative matrix assignments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isobaric-ion-misclassification-documentation))
- **isobaric-overlap-correction-msi**: Use when centroided MSI lipid ion images contain unresolved isobaric overlaps — [X+2:Y+3][M+H]+ overlapped with [X:Y][M+Na]+, or Type II same-class double-bond isobars (e.g. PC 34:0 vs PC 34:1) — that cannot be mass-resolved (Δm/z 0.0025 needs resolution ≥600,000). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isobaric-overlap-correction-msi))
- **isobaric-peptide-abundance-deconvolution**: Use when middle-down (or top-down) histone MS/MS data contain co-fragmenting isobaric polypeptide species sharing the same sequence but different PTM localizations (e.g. H3K18ac vs H3K23ac), and per-localization relative abundances are needed for PTM cross-talk analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/isobaric-peptide-abundance-deconvolution))
- **isobaric-peptide-cofragmentation-splitting**: Use when MS/MS spectra of histone tails (middle-down/top-down) contain co-eluting isobaric peptides that share the same sequence but differ in PTM localization, and each species must be apportioned a share of the composite spectrum's total ion intensity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/isobaric-peptide-cofragmentation-splitting))
- **isobaric-peptide-fragment-ion-ratio-correction**: Use when middle-down or top-down histone MS/MS data contain isobaric polypeptide species that co-fragment — i.e., identical peptide sequences with distinct PTM localizations (e.g., K4me3 vs K14ac positional isomers) — and relative abundances per localization are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/isobaric-peptide-fragment-ion-ratio-correction))
- **isobaric-peptide-fragment-ratio-quantification**: Use when quantifying co-isolated, co-fragmented isobaric histone peptide forms (same sequence, distinct PTM localizations) from middle-down or top-down ETD MS/MS spectra, where XIC or spectral counting is inadequate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/isobaric-peptide-fragment-ratio-quantification))
- **isobaric-peptide-qc-score-filtering**: Use when TMT/iTRAQ PSM quantification tables are impaired by precursor co-isolation (ratio compression) and you need to remove inaccurate peptide quantifications at MS2 or MS3 level before protein-level aggregation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/isobaric-peptide-qc-score-filtering))
- **isobaric-quantification-cross-tool-benchmarking**: Use when validating that a new or custom reporter-ion quantification tool produces intensities consistent with established software (FragPipe, MaxQuant) on isobarically-labeled TMT/TMTpro data acquired at MS2 or MS3 level. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/isobaric-quantification-cross-tool-benchmarking))
- **isobaric-reporter-ion-quantification**: Use when quantifying TMT/TMTpro/iTRAQ reporter ions from mzML spectra plus PSM hits, or when filtering isobarically-labeled scans suffering precursor interference/compression prior to protein-level quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/isobaric-reporter-ion-quantification))
- **isobaric-reporter-quantification-workflow**: Use when TMT, TMTpro, or iTRAQ mzML runs must be searched, quantified at the reporter-ion level, filtered for co-isolation interference, aggregated to proteins, and tested for condition-level abundance changes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/workflows/isobaric-reporter-quantification))
- **isobaric-tmt-peptide-quantification**: Use when quantifying TMT, TMTpro, or iTRAQ reporter ions from mzML files with a search-engine PSM table, especially when co-isolation interference may distort ratios and scan-level filtering is desired. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/isobaric-tmt-peptide-quantification))
- **isobutane-ci-fragmentation-evaluation**: Decision support for choosing a GC-MS ionization configuration (isobutane CI vs methane CI vs EI at 70 or 15 eV) when metabolites fragment excessively or the [M+H]+ isotopic envelope is too weak for isotopologue quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isobutane-ci-fragmentation-evaluation))
- **isocast**: Polymarket weather-market bucket-transition signals for AI agents: per-signal spot checks and prepaid signal bundles by city. Pay per call in USDC via x402 on Base, no signup. ([modbender/skill-library-mcp](https://github.com/modbender/skill-library-mcp/tree/HEAD/data/isocast) · ★21)
- **isochrone-map**: Make travel-time (isochrone) areas and maps on foot, by bike, by car or by transit from sites, stations or venues. Trigger on: isochrone, walkshed, drive time. ([AcropolisDocs-Networks/geo-mapping-marketplace](https://github.com/AcropolisDocs-Networks/geo-mapping-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/geo-mapping-acropolisdocs/skills/isochrone-map))
- **isodesk**: Drive a Windows GUI app (WinUI 3, WPF, WinForms) in an isolated isodesk desktop session on this machine, through UI Automation, without touching the user's own desktop and with no setup: launch it, read its control tree, invoke controls, measure and screenshot to verify. Use for quick checks while developing - confirming a UI change really renders, reproducing a GUI bug, comparing two controls' size or alignment, checking adaptive layout at several window sizes, auditing a screen - and for proving the running binary matches your build. It cannot type, send shortcuts, drag, hover or open file… ([aadhil-kh/isodesk](https://github.com/aadhil-kh/isodesk/tree/HEAD/plugins/isodesk/skills/isodesk) · ★1)
- **isodump**: Use this skill when the user wants to use isodump, cli tool: isodump. ([javimosch/supercli](https://github.com/javimosch/supercli/tree/HEAD/plugins/isodump/skills/quickstart) · ★58)
- **isoform**: Work with Isoform context and tasks handed over from its computer or phone app. Use for activity digests, recalling past work, finding recurring blockers, and carrying out an Isoform one-time task, chat turn, or playbook with its supplied scope and result contract. ([yubozhao/isoform](https://github.com/yubozhao/isoform/tree/HEAD/yansu-agent/isoformskill) · ★31)
- **isoform-abundance-assignment**: Use when RNA-seq data contain circRNA back-spliced junctions detected by CIRCexplorer2/CIRI2/UROBORUS and you need isoform-level relative expression (read counts, FPKM, TPM) rather than locus-level junction counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/isoform-abundance-assignment))
- **isoform-abundance-cosine-similarity-comparison**: Use when benchmarking isoform detection and quantification accuracy of long-read transcript tools against a set of known reference isoforms (e.g. SIRV spike-ins with PacBio data), needing detection counts plus cosine similarity of abundance profiles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/isoform-abundance-cosine-similarity-comparison))
- **isoform-abundance-quantification**: Use when a GMAP- or minimap2-aligned BAM from long-read (PacBio Iso-Seq or ONT) data needs per-isoform abundance quantification for a single gene/locus, and the read-threshold parameter must be tuned or its sensitivity characterized against a known isoform profile. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/isoform-abundance-quantification))
- **isoform-abundance-quantification-comparison**: Use when you have simulated read sets mapped to a known allele database and need to validate fractional allele composition calls against an established isoform/abundance quantification baseline such as Kallisto. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/isoform-abundance-quantification-comparison))
- **isoform-accuracy-benchmarking**: Use when comparing transcript-reconstruction tools on targeted Iso-Seq data and you need quantitative accuracy: simulated precision/recall vs ground-truth isoforms, or fraction of predicted positions supported by &gt;=2 agreeing Illumina reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/isoform-accuracy-benchmarking))
- **isoform-ambiguity-aware-colocalization-analysis**: Use when region sets are defined only by genomic positions without clear isoform association (e.g., POSTAR3/RBP or RNA modification sites) and you need transcriptome-level association testing against picked regions like 5'UTR, CDS, or stop codons. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/isoform-ambiguity-aware-colocalization-analysis))
- **isoform-ambiguity-fractional-overlap-scoring**: Use when a region set (e.g. m6A sites from POSTAR3/miCLIP-seq) has genomic positions but no known transcript isoform association, and you need an overlap score or permutation test against transcriptome regions of interest such as stop codons. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/isoform-ambiguity-fractional-overlap-scoring))
- **isoform-ambiguity-handling**: Use when transcriptome elements (e.g. POSTAR3 RBP sites or miCLIP-seq m6A sites) have known genomic positions but no clear isoform association, and a permutation-based colocalization test at the transcriptome level is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/isoform-ambiguity-handling))
- **isoform-assembly-benchmark-reproduction**: Use when reproducing the Table 3 '1' vs '1-fixed' comparison on real SPIso-seq contigs: you have a cloudrnaSPAdes contig FASTA plus a barcode-to-gene assignment table and must measure how many ambiguously assigned contigs a duplicate-barcode filter removes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/isoform-assembly-benchmark-reproduction))
- **isoform-assignment-parsing-from-alignment-tools**: Use when comparing de novo transcriptome assemblers by counting uniquely assigned reference isoforms from IsoQuant assignment output files against a GENCODE annotation for a specific gene locus. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/isoform-assignment-parsing-from-alignment-tools))
- **isoform-catalog-filtering**: Use when a raw PacBio Iso-Seq isoform annotation (transcript FASTA, abundance table, GFF, ORF FASTA) must be reduced to full-length, high-confidence isoforms via exon-count, truncation, and abundance filters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/isoform-catalog-filtering))
- **isoform-co-expression-clustering**: Use when you have a filtered single-cell isoform-level expression matrix and need noise-robust isoform-to-isoform co-expression estimates grouped into co-usage modules across cell types, prior to DIU/coDIU analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/isoform-co-expression-clustering))
- **isoform-collapse-behavior-benchmarking**: Decision support for choosing an isoform collapsing/merging tool when 5'/3' end variability across samples matters — e.g. simulated or real truncated isoforms sharing the same intron chain must be collapsed and you must know if start/end coordinates survive. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/isoform-collapse-behavior-benchmarking))
- **isoform-collapsing-long-read-rna**: Use when you have splice-aligned FLNC reads (SAM from minimimap2) in a long-read isoform sequencing workflow and need a nonredundant isoform set (GTF, representative FASTA, group file) for downstream QC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/isoform-collapsing-long-read-rna))
- **isoform-contig-removal-rate-quantification**: Use when you have cloudrnaSPAdes assembled contigs (FASTA) plus a barcode-to-gene assignment table and need to measure what fraction of ambiguous barcode assignments a filtering script removes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/isoform-contig-removal-rate-quantification))
- **isoform-coordinate-quantification**: Use when long-read (PacBio/ONT) alignments in BAM, GFF3, or BED6 format for one or a few genes must be converted into consensus splice sites and quantified isoforms without prior knowledge of splice sites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/isoform-coordinate-quantification))
- **isoform-count-filtering**: Use when preparing a transcript-level TPM expression matrix (e.g. GTEx V7 trId/geneId table) for multivariate sQTL mapping and you must keep only genes expressed with at least two isoforms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/isoform-count-filtering))
- **isoform-count-matrix-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating long-read (Nanopore) isoform quantification methods on single-cell or spatially barcoded data where an independent short-read ground-truth cell barcode/UMI matrix is available for comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/isoform-count-matrix-benchmarking))
- **isoform-count-table-processing**: Use when per-cell-type long-read isoform count tables (IsoQuantOutput-style 'Modality-ModalityID.csv', NumModalityPerCluster files, or an AllInfo file) must be converted into pairwise differential isoform expression results with dPI and FDR statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/isoform-count-table-processing))
- **isoform-depth-threshold-verification**: Use when reproducing or validating IsoCon sensitivity curves on simulated PacBio Iso-Seq read sets (e.g., Supplementary Fig. 1) to determine capture depth thresholds per isoform or gene family. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/isoform-depth-threshold-verification))
- **isoform-detection-and-quantification**: Use when low-throughput long-read (PacBio/ONT) data mapped to a single gene or locus needs isoform detection and quantification without prior splice-site knowledge, e.g. PAX6/TP53 PacBio UHR analysis at a 3% read threshold. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/isoform-detection-and-quantification))
- **isoform-detection-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating or comparing isoform detection/quantification performance of long-read transcript tools against a known isoform annotation (e.g., SIRVs), or reproducing the VIsoQLR benchmark where VIsoQLR detects ~72% of isoforms with 99% exon coordinate accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/isoform-detection-benchmarking))
- **isoform-dominant-transcript-calling**: Use when you have a gene-by-transcript TPM expression matrix from a case/control RNA-seq dataset and need to identify per-sample dominant transcript isoforms and derive disease-specific isoform switching events. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/isoform-dominant-transcript-calling))
- **isoform-exon-structure-counting**: Use when a genome-guided transcriptome assembly (StringTie GTF from merged PacBio+Illumina BAMs) must be characterized per-isoform at a specific locus by parsing exon features and counting exons per transcript ID. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/isoform-exon-structure-counting))
- **isoform-expression-count-aggregation**: Use when you need per-sample isoform-switch frequencies or isoform-expression prevalence counts from CanIsoNet's precomputed dMDT tables, e.g. verifying paper-reported values like '21% of samples' or '11 of 39 samples' for a specific transcript pair. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/isoform-expression-count-aggregation))
- **isoform-level-te-count-comparison**: Use when evaluating whether a deep-learning TE quantifier recovers locus- and isoform-specific counts (e.g., 300 bp simulated Alu repeats, simple repeats, HERVK-int isoform pairs) against a known simulated ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/isoform-level-te-count-comparison))
- **isoform-path-extraction-from-assembly-graph**: Use when assembling full-length isoforms reference-free from barcoded RNA-seq linked reads: after per-barcode subgraph simplification (tip clipping, bulge removal, gap closing) you need to read out simple isoform paths per read cloud. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/isoform-path-extraction-from-assembly-graph))
- **isoform-proportion-psi-estimation**: Use when you have per-sample, per-allele inclusion/skipping junction counts (IJC_REF/SJC_REF/IJC_ALT/SJC_ALT) with effective isoform lengths from a merged PAIRADISE count table, and need psi estimation plus an allelic-difference p-value aggregated across individuals. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/isoform-proportion-psi-estimation))
- **isoform-proteoform-abundance-estimation**: Use when peptide-spectrum matches from Morpheus contain peptides shared across multiple proteins, and abundance estimates are needed for protein families or isoforms/proteoforms rather than only individually resolved proteins. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/isoform-proteoform-abundance-estimation))
- **isoform-quantification-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating how accurately a long-read isoform detection/quantification tool recovers and quantifies known transcripts from aligned PacBio/ONT reads, using spike-in references like SIRVs as ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/isoform-quantification-benchmarking))
- **isoform-rich-contig-group-selection**: Use when benchmarking a transcriptome chimera-removal pipeline (e.g. Bellerophon filter orders) and you need a targeted, chimera-rich test set built from isoform-rich contig groups rather than the whole assembly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/isoform-rich-contig-group-selection))
- **isoform-sequence-extraction-and-formatting**: Use when after completing differential isoform expression analysis using IsoformSwitchAnalyzer or equivalent isoform quantification, when you have identified sets of differentially expressed isoforms and need to perform functional characterization through sequence-based homology and structural. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isoform-sequence-extraction-and-formatting))
- **isoform-splice-junction-comparison**: Use when a long-read isoform catalog (e.g., PacBio/IsoPops DB .rds) must be benchmarked against public annotations (RefSeq, UCSC Genes GTF) by intron-chain matching to measure database recovery fractions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/isoform-splice-junction-comparison))
- **isoform-structural-classification**: Use when you have a collapsed representative isoform FASTA (e.g., all.collapsed.rep.fa from cDNA_Cupcake's collapse_isoforms_by_sam.py) and need SQANTI3 structural-category assignments and per-sample retained-isoform counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/isoform-structural-classification))
- **isoform-structure-annotation**: Use when when you have a GTF genome annotation and need to catalog all transcript isoforms and local alternative splicing event variants (exon skipping, intron retention, alternative splice sites, mutually exclusive exons, alternative first/last exons) before quantifying their inclusion levels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/isoform-structure-annotation))
- **isoform-structure-comparison-across-tools**: Use when two long-read isoform callers have been run on the same alignments and you must reconcile their call sets — e.g., quantifying outer-boundary extensions or missing central exons between VIsoQLR and StringTie2 isoform coordinates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/isoform-structure-comparison-across-tools))
- **isoform-structure-redundancy-quantification**: Use when a transcript annotation (GTF) or collapsed long-read isoform set needs redundancy profiling — e.g. testing whether intron chains uniquely identify isoforms before matching PB.X.Y IDs across samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/isoform-structure-redundancy-quantification))
- **isoform-switch-detection**: Use when you have Salmon quant.sf transcript quantifications and phenotype groups, and need to identify isoform switches (changes in isoform usage between conditions) plus functional consequences of the switched isoforms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isoform-switch-detection))
- **isoform-switch-frequency-quantification**: Use when you need the per-sample prevalence of an isoform switching event in a CanIsoNet disease dataset (PCAWG cancers or the OIH, Alzheimer's, Parkinson's, Behcet's RNA-Seq datasets), e.g. 'what fraction of AD samples switch HSPH1-001 to HSPH1-002?' ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/isoform-switch-frequency-quantification))

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isomer-resolution-in-spectral-clustering))
- **isomer-separation-analysis**: Use when an LC–MS/MS dataset shows a single precursor m/z (e.g. m/z 589.31) with multiple chromatographic peaks, and the goal is to count distinct isomers, assign them to spectral clusters, and annotate them via GNPS library spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isomer-separation-analysis))
- **isomer-separation-feature-counting**: Use when an LC–MS/MS data set contains multiple isomeric or co-eluting compounds at the same m/z and you need to quantify how many distinct features survive processing, or compare feature/node consolidation between NP3 and GNPS FBMN. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isomer-separation-feature-counting))

- **isomorphism-check**: 同型原理（Isomorphism Principle）に特化したコード一貫性チェックを実行する。 AIが生成したコードがコードベース既存の「同じ役割・同じ形」の実装と一致しているか検証する。 diffでは見えない比較対象を自動で探し出し、実装の形が揃っているかをチェックする。 以下のトリガーで自動発動: - 「同型原理チェック」「同型チェック」「形が揃ってるか確認」 - 「他の箇所と同じ書き方か確認」「既存パターンと合わせて」 - 「isomorphism check」「isomorphism-check」 - /isomorphism-check [ファイルパスまたはディレクトリ] ([oto1720/claude-agents-skills](https://github.com/oto1720/claude-agents-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/isomorphism-check) · ★1)
- **isomorphism-review**: コードベース全体の実装パターンとの一貫性を検証するレビューを実行する。 「プリンシプル オブ プログラミング」の7設計原則（同型・対称・階層・線形・単純・明証・安全）に照らして AIが生成した局所最適なコードがコードベース全体のパターンから逸脱していないか検証する。 diffだけでは見えない「本来比較すべき箇所」を自動的に探し出し、実装の一貫性レポートを出力する。 以下のトリガーで自動発動: - 「同型原理チェックして」「パターン一貫性を確認して」「コードベースと整合性があるか見て」 - 「AIが書いたコードの一貫性を確認」「既存パターンと比べて」「isomorphism review」 - 「実装スタイルが統一されているか確認」「他の箇所と同じ書き方になってるか」 - 「設計原則チェック」「7原則に照らしてレビュー」 - /isomorphism-review [ファイルパスまたはディレクトリ] ([oto1720/claude-agents-skills](https://github.com/oto1720/claude-agents-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/isomorphism-review) · ★1)
