([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotope-mass-offset-enumeration) · 3 copies)
- **isotope-mass-table-reference-management**: Use when when building a mass spectrometry analysis pipeline that requires exact mass lookups, molecular formula validation, or isotope abundance predictions. Specifically: (1) you are constructing a MolecularFormula class that needs to validate element symbols against known isotopes; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotope-mass-table-reference-management))
- **isotope-matched-formula-confirmation**: Use when assigning molecular formulas to ultrahigh-resolution mass spectra (FT-ICR/Orbitrap) where 13C and 34S polyisotopic peaks must be separated from monoisotopic masses before or after formula assignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotope-matched-formula-confirmation))
- **isotope-natural-abundance-correction-matrix-construction**: Use when correcting LC-MS isotope-labeling FAM data and you need the theoretical NRE correction matrix built from compound formula, isotope natural abundances, and tracer isotopic purity (e.g., 0.99 atomic for U-13C6 glucose). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotope-natural-abundance-correction-matrix-construction))
- **isotope-pattern-analysis-evaluation**: Use when reproducing or extending molecular formula assignment benchmarks on annotated compound sets — e.g. comparing SIRIUS 3.0 vs SIRIUS 4 or isotope-pattern-only vs MS1+MS/MS settings on Supplementary Fig. 6a / Supplementary Table 3 data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotope-pattern-analysis-evaluation))
- **isotope-pattern-and-adduct-assignment**: Use when after feature detection has produced a TSV feature table (from Asari or equivalent) containing m/z, retention time, and intensity columns, and before MS1 or MS2 annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotope-pattern-and-adduct-assignment) · 3 copies)
- **isotope-pattern-annotation-in-feature-detection**: Use when when performing feature detection on centroided DDA mzML files from LC- or GC-HRMS and you need to confirm the elemental composition or differentiate between candidate features—particularly for PFAS screening where isotopic signatures (chlorine, bromine, fluorine) are diagnostic. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotope-pattern-annotation-in-feature-detection) · 2 copies)
- **isotope-pattern-based-formula-ranking**: Use when you have high-resolution LC-MS/MS peak lists for small molecules and need to rank molecular formula candidates for the precursor ion, especially when MS1 isotope patterns are available or the element alphabet should be restricted. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotope-pattern-based-formula-ranking))
- **isotope-pattern-calculation**: Use when quantifying targeted metabolites or isotope-tracer-labelled isotopologues in LC-MS data and a theoretical isotope envelope (e.g. up to six 13C labels) is needed to match and score detected peaks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotope-pattern-calculation))
- **isotope-pattern-detection**: Use when you have MS1-format mass spectrometry files and need to determine the isotope count for peptide features without manual inspection or expert parameter tuning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotope-pattern-detection))
- **isotope-pattern-detection-in-compound-spectra**: Use when annotating LC/MS peak lists (e.g. xsAnnotate objects from faahKO/MM39 processing) and you must identify isotope clusters and charge states within each pseudospectrum rather than treating isotope peaks as separate compounds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotope-pattern-detection-in-compound-spectra))
- **isotope-pattern-match-scoring**: Use when comparing synthetic or real LC-MS/MS runs of known molecules (e.g. nucleosides Cm, m3C, m5C) to assess how mass-accuracy errors (ppm offsets) affect isotope-pattern match scores and quantification quality. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotope-pattern-match-scoring))
- **isotope-pattern-matching-and-filtering**: Use when you have high-resolution centroided mzML files from Orbitrap or similar instruments, a target compound list with known formulas and monoisotopic m/z values, and need to quantify isotopologue abundances (M+0, M+1, M+2, etc.) for stable isotope labeling experiments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotope-pattern-matching-and-filtering))
- **isotope-pattern-mz-generation**: Use when building a compound database for m/z annotation in untargeted metabolomics and you need each compound's neutral mass expanded to adduct and isotope variants — e.g. before searching over 30 databases with MetaboShiny's MetaDBparse. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotope-pattern-mz-generation))
- **isotope-pattern-overlay-visualization**: Use when inspecting PFΔScreen-prioritized suspect-screening hits whose accurate mass matched within 4 mDa, to visually confirm that the experimental MS1 isotopologue envelope agrees with the theoretical pattern before accepting a candidate PFAS annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotope-pattern-overlay-visualization))
- **isotope-pattern-prediction-from-molecular-formula**: Use when defining target metabolites for MS-based targeted metabolic or stable-isotope tracer analysis and you need theoretical isotope patterns and per-isotopologue m/z ranges from a formula/adduct/resolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotope-pattern-prediction-from-molecular-formula))
- **isotope-pattern-scoring**: Use when you have high-resolution MS1 isotope peak lists (masses and abundances) and need to rank candidate molecular formulas by how well their theoretical isotope patterns explain the observed intensities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotope-pattern-scoring))
- **isotope-pattern-similarity-scoring**: Use when multiple peaks are detected in an EIC for a targeted metabolite (or its isotopologues) and the correct peak must be selected using isotope-pattern agreement rather than intensity alone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotope-pattern-similarity-scoring))
- **isotope-pattern-simulation**: Use when a molecular formula annotation (e.g. from SIRIUS/ZODIAC) needs validation from high-resolution MS1 isotope peaks, or when a diagnostic elemental signature such as ~50:50 79Br/81Br must be checked. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotope-pattern-simulation))
- **isotope-pattern-spectral-matching**: Use when when you have LC/MS feature data with observed m/z and intensity values across multiple peaks (monoisotopic and isotopologues) and need to narrow candidate annotations from a metabolite database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotope-pattern-spectral-matching) · 2 copies)
- **isotope-peak-annotation-lcms**: Use when an LC/MS peak list needs grouping of same-analyte features and ion-species annotation (isotopes/adducts) to yield quasi-molecular ions for compound identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotope-peak-annotation-lcms))
- **isotope-peak-deisotoping**: Use when pre-processing imzML mass spectrometry imaging data and you need to remove 13C isotope peaks (M+1, M+2) from a feature table before lipid annotation or downstream statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotope-peak-deisotoping))
- **isotope-peak-exclusion**: Use when generating pseudo MS/MS spectra from MS1-only LC-MS data (mzML/mzXML) or imaging data (imzML) with ms1-id, before writing correlated ion clusters to pseudo_msms.mgf, to strip isotope peaks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotope-peak-exclusion))
- **isotope-peak-filtering**: Use when importing centroided MS2 spectra (txt, msp, or mgf exported from MS-DIAL, or mgf from MZmine) into a DIATAGeR DIADataObj and before LipidIdentifier/ScoreLipids TG annotation, to strip isotopes and noise peaks from multiplexed DIA spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotope-peak-filtering))
- **isotope-peak-grouping**: Use when processing raw LC-HRMS2 chromatograms in MZmine (mzXML/mzML input) and adduct/isotope peaks must be collapsed into single features prior to filtering and .mgf export. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotope-peak-grouping))
- **isotope-ratio-quantification**: Use when a spatial metabolomics peak table (X/Y pixel coordinates plus m/z ion columns, e.g. AFADESI mouse brain data) needs an isotopic peak ratio quality metric, or when candidate M+1 isotopic pairs must be validated by spatial intensity correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotope-ratio-quantification))
- **isotope-site-sync**: Sync a production isotopeai.in build back into the local isotope-code repo. Use when the user says the live site updated and wants new assets fetched, checked, renamed to local hash names, patched across references (index.html, sw.js, JS import strings), replaced into public/, and verified. Covers Obscura-rendered crawling of the SPA, content diffing, role-based hash renaming, reference patching, service worker list updates, and smoke testing. ([Suydev/isotope-code](https://github.com/Suydev/isotope-code/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/isotope-site-sync) · ★1)
- **isotope-tracer-extraction**: Use when you need to quantify target metabolites and their isotopologues from MS-based stable isotope tracer experiments, especially to compare observed vs. theoretical isotope patterns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotope-tracer-extraction))
- **isotope-tracing**: Measure stable isotope tracing and labelling for flux analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/tests/fixtures/router_collection/leaves/isotope-tracing) · ★16)
- **isotope-tracing-data-annotation**: Use when you have a preprocessed LC-MS feature table (e.g. from asari 1.9.2) and need to group degenerate ions (isotopologues/adducts) into empirical compounds with inferred neutral masses, especially for 13C isotope tracing experiments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotope-tracing-data-annotation))
- **isotope-tracing-uptake-vs-synthesis-interpretation**: Use when you have normalized, natural-abundance-corrected isotopologue distributions of fatty acids from 13C6-glucose tracer experiments and need to decide whether apparent label reflects de novo synthesis from glucose-derived acetyl-CoA or uptake of pre-existing fatty acids. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotope-tracing-uptake-vs-synthesis-interpretation))
- **isotopic-abundance-spectrum-computation**: Use when converting a predicted probability distribution over molecular fragments (subformulae or atom subsets with known exact atomic-composition masses) into a discrete mass spectrum at a chosen integer-Dalton or sub-Dalton resolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopic-abundance-spectrum-computation))
- **isotopic-artifact-aware-tolerance-handling**: Use when a GC/EI-MS analyte spectrum matches no reference library and a large enumerated derivative database (e.g., ~422M cathinone-like compounds with precomputed exact masses) must be narrowed to candidates near the analyte's molecular ion M+ mass. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopic-artifact-aware-tolerance-handling))
- **isotopic-distribution-comparison-between-sample-conditions**: Use when you have XCMS peak-picked feature data from paired unlabeled/labeled samples (e.g. 12C vs 13C glucose) in an xcmsSet object and need to flag which features show isotopic enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopic-distribution-comparison-between-sample-conditions))
- **isotopic-distribution-scoring**: Use when you need to quantify how well saturation repair reconstructed the isotopic profile of detector-saturated high-abundance ions in LC–DT IM-MS data, by comparing repaired versus theoretical isotope envelopes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopic-distribution-scoring))
- **isotopic-distribution-shift-analysis**: Use when an XCMS peak table from an untargeted LC/MS stable-isotope experiment must be scanned for features whose m/z peaks are new or significantly more intense in labeled vs unlabeled samples (e.g. 13C-glucose vs 12C-glucose classes). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopic-distribution-shift-analysis))
- **isotopic-enrichment-calculation**: Use when a measured FAM vector (fractional abundances of M+0..M+Nt isotopologs) from a stable isotope labeling LC-MS experiment must be corrected for natural abundance and tracer isotopic impurity and converted to an MDV plus a single enrichment scalar per compound. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopic-enrichment-calculation))
- **isotopic-enrichment-correction**: Use when LC-MS isotopic labeling data (FAM vectors, e.g. XLSX per sample) from high-resolving-power instruments like the Orbitrap must be corrected for natural abundance and tracer isotopic impurity to obtain MDV and enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopic-enrichment-correction))
- **isotopic-enrichment-envelope-simulation**: Use when you need theoretical isotopic envelopes or fine structures for a user-defined peptide sequence or chemical formula at arbitrary 13C enrichment (e.g. 1.07% natural abundance vs ~52% SIP-labeled), or theoretical b/y fragment ion envelopes for PSM annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopic-enrichment-envelope-simulation))
- **isotopic-enrichment-quantification**: Use when you have MS2 spectra from peptides with known stable isotope enrichment (e.g., 52% ¹³C incorporation), a target peptide sequence, and you need to determine which B and Y ion fragments carry how many isotope labels at each position. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotopic-enrichment-quantification) · 2 copies)
- **isotopic-enrichment-simulation**: Use when you have a peptide sequence and need to predict its theoretical m/z values under partial or complete isotopic enrichment (e.g., 1% natural 13C abundance, 50% 13C labeling, or other SIP conditions). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotopic-enrichment-simulation) · 2 copies)
- **isotopic-envelope-calculation**: Use when when you have one or more peptide sequences (as strings) and need to predict their theoretical isotopic distribution for comparison against experimental MS peaks, validation of mass calibration, or simulation of expected peptide signals in a mass spectrometry assay. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotopic-envelope-calculation))
- **isotopic-envelope-comparison**: Use when untargeted LC/MS feature tables from pairs of biologically equivalent samples (one grown on a 12C substrate, one on a 13C-labeled substrate) must be screened for isotope-label-driven shifts in isotopic envelope. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopic-envelope-comparison))
- **isotopic-envelope-generation-at-fixed-enrichment**: Use when when you have a peptide sequence, MS2 fragment charge states, and a known isotope enrichment fraction (e.g., 1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotopic-envelope-generation-at-fixed-enrichment) · 2 copies)
- **isotopic-envelope-prediction**: Use when processing high-resolution (HRMS) GC-CI-MS stable-isotope labeling data (e.g. GC-Orbitrap) and you need the theoretical [M+H]+ isotopologue m/z values, abundances, and resolvability for a derivatized formula before quantifying isotopologues with isoSCAN. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopic-envelope-prediction))
- **isotopic-envelope-visualization**: Use when you have a raw MS run (e.g. PXD000001, TMT experiment from a Thermo Orbitrap Q Exactive) and need scan-level visual exploration: heat maps, 3D scan maps, or isotopic-envelope zooms, in R. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/isotopic-envelope-visualization))
- **isotopic-fine-structure-calculation**: Use when you need theoretical isotope patterns or carbon-number estimates for molecular formulas — e.g., validating R13C ratios from IDSL.IPA peaklists or assigning elemental compositions from MS1 data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopic-fine-structure-calculation))
- **isotopic-fine-structure-monte-carlo-simulation**: Use when you need theoretical isotopic fine-structure peaks (m/z + relative abundance) for a compound or peptide at natural abundance or partial SIP enrichment, especially where the FFT envelope method cannot resolve fine structure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopic-fine-structure-monte-carlo-simulation))
- **isotopic-impurity-accounting**: Use when when analyzing LC-MS data from stable isotope labeling experiments where measured isotopologue abundances are contaminated by naturally occurring isotopes and tracer isotopic impurity, and you have access to unlabeled sample reference measurements to empirically model these confounding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotopic-impurity-accounting) · 2 copies)
- **isotopic-labeling-pattern-validation**: Use when a 1H-13C HSQC (pseudo-2D) isotopic-labeling dataset must be decomposed into isotopic species fractions and checked against a predicted labeling pattern from a standard of known composition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopic-labeling-pattern-validation))
- **isotopic-pattern-detection-and-merging**: Use when your peak table contains features suspected of being artifacts of incorrect isotopic pattern splitting during preprocessing—particularly when you observe multiple ions with very similar mass-to-charge ratios and retention times, or when visual inspection of m/z vs. retention time plots. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotopic-pattern-detection-and-merging) · 2 copies)
- **isotopic-pattern-dot-product-scoring**: Use when you have an aligned peak table (.csv of m/z and retention-time pairs) from an HRMS non-targeted analysis and need to annotate features with molecular formulae and confirm identifications via isotopic-pattern and MS/MS matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopic-pattern-dot-product-scoring))
- **isotopic-pattern-matching**: Use when after feature detection has produced candidate formula–adduct pairs and their corresponding m/z values, but before final compound identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotopic-pattern-matching) · 2 copies)
- **isotopic-pattern-mispicking-detection**: Use when you have a peak table from tandem MS preprocessing (e.g., MS-DIAL, Metaboscape) and suspect that isotopic patterns have been incorrectly split during feature detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotopic-pattern-mispicking-detection) · 3 copies)
- **isotopic-pattern-prediction-for-lipids**: Use when MSI lipidomics ion images contain same-class lipid species differing by one double bond (e.g., PC 38:3 vs PC 38:2, PE 38:3 vs PE 38:4) whose monoisotopic signals are inflated by the M+2 isotopologue of the more unsaturated isobar. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopic-pattern-prediction-for-lipids))
- **isotopic-pattern-similarity-scoring**: Use when annotating ion peaks from an imaging MS feature matrix (e.g., 14,260 pixels x 3,044 ions) against a metabolite library and you need isotopic-pattern similarity as one of the multi-evidence scores for candidate ranking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopic-pattern-similarity-scoring))
- **isotopic-peak-intensity-distribution**: Use when when you have one or more peptide sequences (as strings) and need to predict their isotopic distribution pattern for MS instrument simulation, peak deconvolution, or validation of observed isotopic envelopes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotopic-peak-intensity-distribution))
- **isotopic-peak-pair-detection**: Use when a pixel x m/z feature matrix from an imaging mass spectrometry run (e.g., AFAD-ESI positive mode) must be classified into isotopic peak pairs and adduct ion pairs ([M+H]+, [M+K]+, [M+Na]+, [M+NH4]+, [M+H-H2O]+) before metabolite annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopic-peak-pair-detection))
- **isotopic-peak-pair-identification**: Use when you have a preprocessed spatial metabolomics peak table (X/Y pixel coordinates plus m/z ion columns) and need to quantify what fraction of ions are isotopic peaks or flag ion pairs whose spatial intensity patterns co-vary. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopic-peak-pair-identification))
- **isotopic-peak-removal**: Use when after loading raw FT-ICR MS peak lists with assigned molecular formulas when you have detected peaks across multiple m/z values that correspond to isotopic variants of the same parent compound. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotopic-peak-removal))
- **isotopic-profile-theoretical-vs-experimental-matching**: Use when annotating untargeted LC/HRMS peaks (mzXML, mzML, netCDF) with molecular formulas from only MS1-level data, especially when database-dependent tools (MetDNA, SIRIUS, ZODIAC) risk missing exposome compounds absent from biochemical databases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopic-profile-theoretical-vs-experimental-matching))
- **isotopic-signature-clustering-and-grouping**: Use when you have a feature table (m/z, drift_time, retention_time, intensity) from LC-IMS-MS or similar multi-dimensional MS acquisition and need to (1) link isotopic variants to their monoisotopic parent features, (2) disambiguate true chemical features from noise or instrumental artifacts, or. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotopic-signature-clustering-and-grouping) · 3 copies)
- **isotopic-signature-detection**: Use when the input is a DEIMoS-aligned multi-sample feature table (e.g. example_alignment.h5) in an N-dimensional space (m/z, drift time, retention time) and the goal is to glean ion charge state or identification evidence from isotopic signatures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopic-signature-detection))
- **isotopic-signature-detection-and-filtering**: Use when after DEIMoS isotope detection has assigned potential isotopic signatures to detected features in aligned MS1 data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotopic-signature-detection-and-filtering) · 3 copies)
- **isotopic-signature-validation-and-filtering**: Use when after isotope detection has enumerated C13 isotopologue patterns across m/z, drift time, and retention time dimensions, and you need to reduce false positives by retaining only well-populated isotopic signature clusters before annotation or export. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotopic-signature-validation-and-filtering) · 4 copies)
- **isotopic-species-quantification-from-hsqc**: Use when a pseudo-2D (e.g. 1H-13C HSQC) isotope-labeling NMR series must be decomposed into individual isotopic species and their relative abundances quantified, especially with overlapping peaks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopic-species-quantification-from-hsqc))
- **isotopic-subnetwork-partitioning**: Use when you have a preprocessed LC-MS feature table (e.g. asari 1.9.2 TSV with feature ID, m/z, rtime, intensities) and need to group adduct/isotope-related features into empirical compounds with inferred neutral M0 masses, without prior formulas. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopic-subnetwork-partitioning))
- **isotopic-trace-peak-shape-filtering**: Use when filtering raw FeatureFinderMetabo output from centroided ddMS2 mzML data to remove spurious or poorly shaped features before blank correction and PFAS prioritization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopic-trace-peak-shape-filtering))
- **isotopically-labeled-lipid-library-qc**: Use when a workflow imports U13C_DT_CCS_library.csv from the FelinaHildebrand/MobiLipid repository and must confirm the 377-value, 162-lipid-species, 15-class curation is intact before internal standardization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopically-labeled-lipid-library-qc))
- **isotopically-nonstationary-metabolic-flux-analysis**: Use when time-course stable-isotope labeling data (e.g., [13C5,15N2] glutamate 0-8 h sampling) have been processed into natural-abundance-corrected isotopologue distributions and fluxes with confidence intervals are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopically-nonstationary-metabolic-flux-analysis))
- **isotopologue-12c-13c-peak-handling**: Use when grouping co-eluting MS1 peaks into CSA clusters from IDSL.IPA peaklists/peak_alignment directories and you must decide whether an m/z-shifted neighbor is a genuine 12C/13C isotopologue of the seed peak. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-12c-13c-peak-handling))
- **isotopologue-abundance-correction**: Use when you have LC-MS data from isotope labeling experiments where FAM measurements must be transformed to MDV values. Specifically, apply this when: (1) you have measured fractional abundances of isotopologues (FAM) in XLSX format from a high-resolution instrument (e.g., Orbitrap); ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotopologue-abundance-correction) · 2 copies)
- **isotopologue-abundance-normalization**: Use when tracer quantification output (e.g. from TarMet's isotope tracer assay on 13C6-glucose samples) provides raw isotopologue peak areas (M+1, M+2, ...) and you need per-metabolite fractional labeling to check pathway-position labeling patterns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-abundance-normalization))
- **isotopologue-adduct-annotation**: Use when processing centroid-mode AIF LC–MS metabolomics chromatograms and you need to assign isotopologues, adducts, and in-source fragments across features, especially when focusing on a statistically significant subset rather than the full dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-adduct-annotation))
- **isotopologue-adduct-cluster-assignment**: Use when after sample alignment and peak picking have produced an aligned feature table with m/z and retention time coordinates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotopologue-adduct-cluster-assignment) · 3 copies)
- **isotopologue-adduct-cross-assay-link-encoding**: Use when after structural cluster assignment and correlation clustering are complete, and you need to represent the full set of structural relationships (isotopologues, adducts, cross-assay links, and correlation co-membership) in a single unified graph for interactive visualization, network. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotopologue-adduct-cross-assay-link-encoding) · 3 copies)
- **isotopologue-adduct-link-identification**: Use when you have selected statistically significant features from multi-assay untargeted LC-MS metabolomics data and need to group them by structural relationships defined by their mass-to-charge ratios (m/z) and retention times (RT). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotopologue-adduct-link-identification) · 2 copies)
- **isotopologue-chromatographic-peak-extraction**: Use when generating a benchmark dataset from user-provided molecules with known composition and retention-time boundaries, requiring isotopologue-level peak extraction from mzML files before NPP reliability assessment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-chromatographic-peak-extraction))
- **isotopologue-correction-method-comparison**: Use when comparing how isotope-correction schemes (MDT, ULS, NRE) perform on FAM/MDV data for labeled metabolites at a stated nominal instrument resolution (e.g. 140,000 or 280,000 Orbitrap), or when choosing MDT vs ULS for a tracer/compound class. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-correction-method-comparison))
- **isotopologue-deconvolution-model-selection**: Use when MS-measured isotopologue timecourses (single or multiple isotope tracers, e.g. 13C UDP-GlcNAc SIRM data) must be deconvoluted into moiety abundances and the -fitting deconvolution model must be chosen from several plausible models. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-deconvolution-model-selection))
- **isotopologue-distribution-fitting**: Use when HRMS data from a transient stable-isotope labeling time course (e.g., [13C5,15N2]glutamine or [U-13C6]glucose) must be converted to isotopologue distributions and used to estimate metabolic fluxes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-distribution-fitting))
- **isotopologue-distribution-matrix-construction**: Use when when you have measured fractional abundances of isotopologues (FAM) from LC-MS in an isotope labeling experiment and need to correct them to obtain true mass distribution vectors (MDV) that reflect only the labeling contribution, accounting for background from naturally occurring isotopes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotopologue-distribution-matrix-construction) · 2 copies)
- **isotopologue-distribution-normalization**: Use when single-cell isotope-tracing data (SpaceM spatiomolecular anndata objects, per-cell ion intensities for fatty acids like palmitate C16:0 and oleate C18:1) must be converted to corrected, normalized isotopologue fractions before binomial-fitting uptake estimates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-distribution-normalization))
- **isotopologue-distribution-simulation**: Use when you have a list of molecular formulae and need their monoisotopic masses plus theoretical isotopologue patterns (including adducts and multiply charged species) to target ions in LC-IM-MS or MS data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-distribution-simulation))
- **isotopologue-envelope-extraction**: Use when khipu has preannotated untargeted metabolomics or stable isotope tracing data (e.g. U-13C E. coli, 548 empCpds from 3,602 asari features) and you need to extract per-compound isotope-index envelopes for downstream labeling analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-envelope-extraction))
- **isotopologue-fractional-abundance-correction**: Use when LC-MS data from isotope labeling experiments (FAM vectors per compound/sample, e.g. XLSX input) must be corrected for natural abundance, tracer isotopic impurity, and resolution effects before deriving MDVs and enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-fractional-abundance-correction))
- **isotopologue-grouping-by-mass-accuracy**: Use when after PuInc_seeker has identified putative incorporations in a stable-isotope-labeled LC/MS dataset (e.g., CELL_Glc12 unlabeled vs. CELL_Glc13 labeled samples). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotopologue-grouping-by-mass-accuracy) · 2 copies)
- **isotopologue-intensity-forward-calculation**: Use when a measured MS isotopologue profile of a multi-moiety metabolite (e.g. single-tracer 13C UDP-GlcNAc timecourses or multi-tracer 13C/18O data) must be reconstructed from moiety state fractional abundances, i.e. computing I_x,calc per isotopologue mass shift from a moiety model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-intensity-forward-calculation))
- **isotopologue-intensity-simulation-from-moiety-models**: Use when you need Monte Carlo benchmarking of how measurement noise propagates through moiety-model-derived isotopologue profiles — e.g. comparing 13C-only vs 13C/18O glucose multi-tracer error behavior at defined sigma levels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-intensity-simulation-from-moiety-models))
- **isotopologue-ion-image-extraction-from-imzml**: Use when MALDI imaging data are stored as imzML and you need ion images for all isotopologues of specific molecules (e.g. fatty acids 20:3, 20:4, 20:5, 22:4, 22:5) from an ion list, rather than only the annotated natural-abundance peaks from METASPACE. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-ion-image-extraction-from-imzml))
- **isotopologue-ion-pairing**: Use when processing untargeted LC/HRMS (mzXML, mzML, netCDF) data where redundant isotope peaks inflate peak lists and only carbon-containing compound signals (12C monoisotopic isotopologues) should be retained. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-ion-pairing))
- **isotopologue-labelling-visualization-of-metabolic-flux**: Use when TarMet has produced a peak-area table of 13C isotopologues for labelled samples (e.g. MRC5 cells labelled 5 or 60 min with 13C6-glucose) and the goal is to visualize labelling patterns across metabolites and assess parallel-sample consistency. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-labelling-visualization-of-metabolic-flux))
- **isotopologue-mass-delta-calculation**: Use when when constructing a reference mass-matching framework for untargeted metabolomics or isotope-tracing LC-MS data, before pattern-matching observed features to isotopic and adduct variants. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotopologue-mass-delta-calculation) · 2 copies)
- **isotopologue-mass-difference-calculation**: Use when building a resolution-dependent isotopologue correction matrix for a compound formula at a given nominal instrument resolution, and you must decide which same-nominal-mass isotopologs (e.g. 17O vs 15N/13C contributions) can be resolved and which correction-matrix columns to zero. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-mass-difference-calculation))
- **isotopologue-mass-offset-calculation**: Use when you have a peak-picked feature table (HDF5 format with m/z, drift_time, retention_time, intensity columns) and need to identify and label isotopic signatures within singly charged species. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/isotopologue-mass-offset-calculation) · 2 copies)
- **isotopologue-mass-shift-detection**: Use when pairwise LC/MS feature tables from an isotope-supplemented condition (e.g. 13C6-leucine) and an unlabeled control must be compared to find labeled features via a specific mass shift, or to test whether particular lipids derive from a labeled precursor. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-mass-shift-detection))
- **isotopologue-pairing-assessment**: Use when processing untargeted LC/HRMS mzXML/mzML/netCDF data with IDSL.IPA and you need to suppress redundant isotope/adduct-like peaks by pairing 12C and 13C signals within each scan, or when troubleshooting why peaks were excluded by nIsoPair/RCS thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-pairing-assessment))
- **isotopologue-pairing-quality-threshold-evaluation**: Use when comparing IDSL.IPA peak lists (e.g. 20,253 peaks for MTBLS1684 file 003.d) against MZmine 2, xcms-CAMERA or MS-DIAL output and you must attribute missing peaks to isotopologue pairing or peak quality thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-pairing-quality-threshold-evaluation))
- **isotopologue-pattern-detection-lcms**: Use when after filtering LC-MS features by statistical significance (e.g., p-value &lt; 0.01) and you wish to group features that represent the same metabolite at different isotopologue states. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotopologue-pattern-detection-lcms) · 2 copies)
- **isotopologue-pattern-interpretation**: Use when a preprocessed feature table contains unexplained ion groups whose members differ by isotope or adduct mass shifts (e.g. 1.0034 Da 13C/12C, 22.9893 Da Na+), or when annotating U-13C-labeled tracing samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-pattern-interpretation))
- **isotopologue-pattern-matching**: Use when performing targeted LC-MS stable-isotope-tracer or isotopologue analysis: you have defined target metabolites, extracted M+H isotopologue EICs per sample, and need to score observed isotope patterns against theoretical patterns at the MS resolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-pattern-matching))
- **isotopologue-peak-quantification**: Use when you have EICs for a targeted isotopologue across multiple tracer samples (e.g. 13C6-glucose labelling) and need retention-time-aligned peak areas per sample for isotopologue quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-peak-quantification))
- **isotopologue-peak-selection-targeted-ms**: Use when quantifying a targeted metabolite and its isotopologues from LC-MS EICs where multiple peaks are detected per isotopologue and the correct target peak must be chosen via isotope-pattern similarity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-peak-selection-targeted-ms))
- **isotopologue-ppm-error-verification**: Use when processing high-resolution (Orbitrap) centroided GC-MS mzML files with isoSCAN autoQ in stable-isotope labeling experiments and you must confirm that the integrated isotopologue peaks correspond to the predicted m/z values within the allowed mass error before reporting enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-ppm-error-verification))
- **isotopologue-profile-normalization**: Use when mass spectrometry isotopologue profiles (e.g. 13C UDP-GlcNAc timecourses) must be reconstructed from moiety state fractional abundances, or when validating deconvolution outputs against measured isotopologue distributions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-profile-normalization))
- **isotopologue-quantification**: Use when centWave-processed LC-MS chromatograms of known target metabolites must be quantified per adduct (M+H, M+Na, M+K) with isotopologue-level confidence, including isotope tracer assays. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-quantification))
- **isotopologue-quantification-data-normalization**: Use when after autoQ has extracted and integrated peak areas for all isotopologues of your targeted compounds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotopologue-quantification-data-normalization))
- **isotopologue-quantification-extraction**: Use when you have GC-CI-MS or LC-MS data in mzML format targeting a defined set of compounds with known monoisotopic mass, retention time, and chemical formula, and you need per-isotopologue (M+0, M+1, M+2, etc.) quantification metrics (area, maxo intensity, abundance). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotopologue-quantification-extraction) · 2 copies)
- **isotopologue-ratio-quality-assessment**: Use when evaluating the reliability of non-targeted data pre-processing (NPP) tools (XCMS, MZmine 2, MS-DIAL, etc.) on known metabolite peaks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotopologue-ratio-quality-assessment))
- **isotopologue-ratio-quality-assessment**: Use when assessing peak-abundance quality of XCMS or MZmine 2 pre-processing output against an mzRAPP benchmark — i.e. quantifying what fraction of matched peaks show degenerated isotopologue ratios. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-ratio-quality-assessment))
- **isotopologue-ratio-quality-filtering**: Use when when extracting isotopologue patterns from centroided mzML files in non-targeted metabolomics workflows, apply this filter after detecting candidate isotopologue peaks but before finalizing the benchmark dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotopologue-ratio-quality-filtering))
- **isotopologue-signature-detection**: Use when you have preprocessed, statistically significant LC-MS features (from multiple assays or a single assay) and need to group features that represent the same metabolite in different isotopic labeling states. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/isotopologue-signature-detection) · 3 copies)
- **isotopologue-timecourse-parameter-optimization**: Use when you need to solve the boundary-value inverse problem of fitting moiety model parameters to MS-measured isotopologue timecourse data, and/or to select among multiple plausible moiety models via AICc. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-timecourse-parameter-optimization))
- **isotopologue-to-nmol-13c-unit-conversion**: Use when isotopologue distributions from a [U-13C6]glucose or similar tracer HRMS time course have already been natural-abundance corrected (NACorrectionFxnII) and the goal is to express enrichment as nmol of 13C-labeled molecules formed per time point for kinetic clustering or precursor-product. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-to-nmol-13c-unit-conversion))
- **isotopologue-trace-confirmation**: Use when building LC-IM-MS (drift tube IM-MS) databases from profile mzML files for a list of molecular formulae, and candidate primary-ion m/z traces must be confirmed by isotopologue matching after RT peak picking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopologue-trace-confirmation))
- **isotopomer-distribution-comparison**: Use when two isotopomer distributions for the same metabolite are available — one from a classical NMR multiplet fit over a restricted isotopomer subset and one from the deep-learning fit over all possible isotopomers — and their agreement must be quantified. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/isotopomer-distribution-comparison))
- **isotype-control-correlation-analysis**: Use when you need to verify that a cell-intrinsic technical noise estimate for droplet-based protein (ADT) data is robust — e.g. correlating fitted background means µ1 with isotype controls or checking k=2 vs k=3 mixture-model concordance before building the dsb technical component. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/isotype-control-correlation-analysis))
- **isovfy**: Use this skill when the user wants to use isovfy, cli tool: isovfy. ([javimosch/supercli](https://github.com/javimosch/supercli/tree/HEAD/plugins/isovfy/skills/quickstart) · ★58)
- **ISP Technical Support Roleplay**: Act as a technical support operator for an Internet Service Provider, handling connectivity inquiries and ticket requests while strictly avoiding AI disclaimers to maintain the persona. ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/ConvSkill/english_gpt3.5_8_GLM4.7/isp-technical-support-roleplay) · ★620 · 4 copies)

- **isp-traffic-hijacking**: 运营商流量劫持深度技术手册。覆盖运营商(ISP)级别的多层级流量劫持技术：DNS层劫持(NXDOMAIN/透明代理/强制重定向)、HTTP层劫持(广告注入/内容篡改/iframe嵌入/状态码劫持)、HTTPS层劫持(SSL剥离/证书替换/CDN边缘劫持)、网络层劫持(BGP劫持/路由操纵/AS路径篡改/前缀劫持)、DPI深度包检测与流量整形、透明代理与缓存投毒、2026最新技术(QUIC劫持/ECH绕过/5G信令劫持/eSIM远程配置劫持)、检测防御矩阵与实战排查SOP。含交互式HTML演示。用于排查"网页被插广告""访问被重定向""HTTPS证书异常""流量异常路由"等运营商级别劫持场景。 ([qinian11/TGSEC-Qtzuu](https://github.com/qinian11/TGSEC-Qtzuu/tree/HEAD/domains/recon/src-methods/_vendor/sinian/sinian-isp-traffic-hijacking) · ★12 · 9 copies)
